Welcome back to the Draft Viewer! Today we've got a *New Phyrexia / Mirrodin Besieged / Scars of Mirrodin* draft from the Top 8 of the [**Magic Online**](https://web.archive.org/web/20140527070414/http://www.wizards.com/Magic/Digital/MagicOnline.aspx) Championship Series Season 6 Draft on **Magic Online**. You can check out the full **Magic Online** PTQ schedule here.

[![](https://media.magic.wizards.com/image_legacy_migration/images/magic/daily/activity/da728_ss.jpg)](https://web.archive.org/web/20110608000000/http://gatherer.wizards.com/magic/draftools/draftviewer.asp?draftid=mtgo2442998)

## top 8 bracket

### Quaterfinals

**goobafish**

Shadow\_Prince

**\_megafone\_**

about2punt

**Avantasian**

Deusex1337

**Sergeis2007**

angelplayer

### Semi-finals

**goobafish, 2-0**

\_megafone\_, 2-0

**Avantasian, 2-1**

Sergeis2007, 2-0

### Finals

**goobafish, 2-0**

Avantasian, 2-1

### Champion

**goobafish, 2-1**
